Implement cross_corr via np.fft.rfft:
```
def cross_lag(w,p):
 n=len(p); L=2*n-1
 r=np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])
 size=1<<((L+n-2)-1).bit_length() # 3n-2
 W=np.fft.rfft(w,size); R=np.fft.rfft(r,size)
 c_all=np.fft.irfft(W*R,size)
 return c_all[n-1:2*n-1] # length n? indices n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n
```

```
def fourier_objective(coeffs):
    if ctx['stop']: raise TimeLimit
    log_base = ctx['basis'] @ coeffs  # if basis without constant
    log_base -= np.max(log_base)
    p = np.exp(log_base); p /= p.sum()
    b=fast_conv(p); M=b.max()
    # target maybe M - avg; use smooth max: H = M + tau * log(mean(exp((b-M)/tau)))
```
We can simply use the max objective (non-smooth) with L-BFGS-B? It expects smooth but can handle nonsmooth maybe poorly. Use smooth logsum: `H = M + tau * np.log(np.mean(np.exp((b-M)/tau)))`. Use p update via log multiplicative:
```
def em_step(p, T, alpha=1.0):
    h = conv(p); hsafe=max(h,eps)
    ratio = minimum(max(T/hsafe, 1e-6), 1e6)
    c = cross_conv(ratio, p)
    c = maximum(c, eps)
    # center log c weighted by p
    lc = log(c)
    lm = average(lc, weights=p) (if p sum=1)
    p2 = p * exp(alpha*(lc - lm))
    normalize
```

   excess = max(0, sum(h-cap_over?)). For non-active, λ = -excess / count_nonactive, if max below after increase <=cap. Check. This gives T easily. If violates cap, adjust via waterfill. Implement robust but not too slow:
```
def project_to_cap(h, cap, L):
    # assumes h sum~1
    hmax=h.max()
    if cap >= hmax: return h.copy()
    if cap <= 1.0/L: return np.full(L,1/L)
    # initial lambda = 0 gives T=clip(h,0,cap); sum_s = sum_s.
    # compute active = h > cap; excess = sum(h[active]) - cap*len(active)
    # remaining count = L - len(active), if L*cap >=1.
    # We need add excess by lowering lambda (negative) uniformly for non-active: lambda = -excess / count_nonactive.
    lam = -excess / count_nonactive
    T = np.clip(h - lam, 0, cap)
    # Check sum; if not 1 or T has >cap? It can't >cap due clip; if some lows clipped 0, sum may be <1 (if lam too negative). Then need more complex: some lows at lower bound require more lambda negative for other? Actually if lam negative increases all, lower bound 0 only if h_i+|lam|<0 impossible since h_i>=0 and lam negative -> h_i - lam >=h_i positive. So lower bound never active for lam negative. Upper bounds may add: if some non-active with h_i + |lam| > cap, they also become active. Our initial active set h_i>cap only; after adding, those just below cap may exceed cap. Need waterfill.
```
Let's do waterfill properly:
We need T_i = min(cap, h_i - λ) for λ likely <=0 (since sum target > sum at clipping? Actually T=clip(h,0,cap) sum<1; to reach sum1, λ<0). For λ<0, lower bound never active because h_i - λ > h_i ≥0. Some entries may hit cap. We can find λ by active waterfill:
- Let active set A where h_i - λ >= cap. Equivalent h_i >= cap + λ. Since λ negative, threshold < cap. Active entries set to cap. Inactive entries h_i - λ.
Sum(λ)= cap*|A(λ)| + ∑_{i∉A} (h_i - λ).
We need sum=1. Monotone in λ? As λ decreases (more negative), more inactive become active; sum? For each inactive, derivative -1; active derivative 0; so sum decreases as λ increases; as λ decreases, sum increases? At λ=0, active h>cap, sum<1. At λ=-big, all active, sum=L*cap >1. So solution between -big and 0. We can bisection 30 iterations. To speed, maybe 20 iterations enough. L=13k, 20*13k=260k ops per project, times 5000=1.3B (numpy vector each iteration, maybe slow 10s per 100? Actually each bisection iteration vector over 13k in C ~0.05ms? 20=1ms, 5000=5s, okay). Fine.
- Need upper bound λ where sum≥1. Start hi=0 (sum≤1). lo = - (h.max() - cap + maybe 1)? At λ=cap - h.max(), all h_i-λ >=0? Wait to make all active cap, need h_min - λ >= cap? Actually all active at λ <= min_i (h_i - cap)? For λ very negative, yes. Use lo = -h_max - 1.0. Sum(lo)=L*cap (since all h_i - lo > cap) >1 if cap>avg. Good.
- Bisection 30. Return T. Normalize small.

Implementation:
```
def project_to_cap(h, cap):
    L=len(h)
    cap = float(cap)
    hmax = float(h.max())
    if cap >= hmax - 1e-18:
        return h.copy()
    avg=1.0/L
    if cap <= avg + 1e-18:
        return np.full(L, avg)
    lo = -(hmax - cap) - 1.0  # maybe enough? To make all h_i - lo > cap: need -lo > hmax? lo=-hmax? let's set lo = -hmax - 1.0
    hi = 0.0
    # ensure sum(lo)>=1
    # function sum_lambda(lam): np.clip(h - lam, 0.0, cap).sum()
    for _ in 40:
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        s=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,cap).sum()
        if s < 1.0:
            hi=mid
        else:
            lo=mid
    T=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,cap)
    s=T.sum()
    if s>1: T/=s
    return T
```
This uses 40 vector ops. Could reduce to 25.

But if cap very close M, T close to h; EM ratio close to 1. Need many iterations. We can choose cap reduction that gives measurable T difference.

def em_step(p, T, alpha=1.0):
    h=fast_conv(p); hsafe=np.maximum(h,1e-300)
    ratio=T/hsafe
    ratio=np.clip(ratio,1e-12,1e12)
    c=cross_lag(ratio,p)
    c=np.maximum(c,1e-300)
    logc=np.log(c)
    # weighted mean maybe by p
    denom=p.sum()
    logc_w = float(np.dot(p, logc)/denom)
    # avoid large: clip logc-logc_w to [-30/alpha? Actually alpha later]
    p2=p*np.exp(alpha*(logc-logc_w))
    p2=np.maximum(p2,0); s=p2.sum(); if s>0 p2/=s
    return p2, h.max()

Let's implement a general `em_target_step(p, T, alphas)` top-level that evaluates a few alphas and returns best p,M.
```
def em_target_step(p,T,alphas):
    h=fast_conv(p); M=h.max(); hsafe=max(h,1e-300); ratio=T/hsafe; ratio=clip

    # scale logc? To make alphas comparable, `step = logc-lm`; `scale = max(abs(step))`; if scale<1e-15 return p,M
    best_p=p; best_M=M
    for al in alphas:
       a = al / scale  # if scale normalized, exponent max al

### Mirror balance implementation in detail

`mirror_balance(p0,limit_s,verbose=True):`
- p=norm; best.

- Each iter:
   b=fast_conv(p); M=b.max(); update best.
   spread = M - np.median(b); if spread < 1e-18: spread = M*1e-6
   tau = tau_factor*spread
   z = (b-M)/tau; z=np.clip(z, -50, 0); w=exp(z); w/=w.sum()
   c=cross_lag(w,p)
   d = c - np.mean(c)  # or weighted mean? For gradient under sum, subtract weighted average? In mirror update p exp(-α d) normalized; any constant irrelevant. For numerical, subtract mean. Weighted mean maybe preserves mass; use weighted `np.average(d, weights=p)`.
   scale=max(abs(d)); if small, no update.
   d=d/scale; best trial = p,M.
   for α in alphas:
      p2=p*exp(-α*d)  # negative because high c reduce
      p2=max + floor? If p zeros, keep zero. Add floor: `p2=np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)` maybe.
      normalize; M2=fast_conv(p2).max(); if M2<best_trial: track.
   if best_trial_M < M*(1-1e-15): p=best_trial; no=0 else no++.
   if no>300: adjust tau_factor; if no>1500: p=norm(best + small noise on best?), reset.

Fourier objective with smooth logsum:
```
def fourier_objective(coeffs):
    ctx = _FOURIER_CTX
    if ctx.get('stop', False): raise _Timeout()
    if time.monotonic() > ctx.get('deadline', np.inf): ctx['stop']=True; raise _Timeout()
    basis=ctx['basis']
    s = basis @ coeffs
    s = s - np.max(s)
    p = np.exp(s); p /= p.sum()
    b=fast_conv(p); M=float(b.max())
    tau=float(ctx['tau'])
    z = tau*b
    zmax=float(z.max())
    z = z - zmax
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 0)
    sumexp = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    H = (zmax + np.log(sumexp)) / tau
    if i am  not finite: return 1e100
    # update best true M if M < ctx['best_M']
    if M < ctx['best_M'] - 1e-16:
        ctx['best_M']=M; ctx['best_p']=p.copy()
    return float(H)
```

```
def em_cap_optimize(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
  deadline=...
  p=norm(p0); best=p.copy(); best_M=fast_conv(p).max(); n=len(p); L=2n-1; avg=1.0/L
  caps_mults=[0.999,0.998,0.997,0.995,0.99,0.985,0.98,0.97,0.95,0.93,0.90,0.85,0.80]
  it=0; no_improve=0
  for mult in caps_mults:
     cap = max(avg*1.001, best_M*mult)
     # If cap not lower than current best_M by at least 1e-7, cap=best_M*(1-1e-6)?
     while time < deadline:
        h=fast_conv(p); M=h.max()
        if M < best_M - 1e-16: best...; no=0
        # cap_target based on current M maybe:
        cap_cur = max(avg*1.001, M*mult)
        if M <= cap_cur*(1+1e-8): break
        T=project_to_cap(h, cap_cur)
        # If T is too close to h (max diff<1e-15), cap_cur lower? For tight mult, if no change, break to next mult.
        p2,M2 = em_target_step(p,T,[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8])
        if M2 < M - 1e-16:
           p=p2; no=0
        else:
           no+=1
           if no > 100: break
        it+=1
        if verbose and it%200==0: print
  return best,best_M
```
This uses target T from current h each iteration. If cap_cur achieved for that mult (M <= M*mult impossible unless mult=1), Wait cap_cur = M*mult < M always, so condition M <= cap_cur never true unless M reduced below cap_cur? But cap_cur recalculated from current M each iter; M*mult always less than M, so we will always try reduce by mult of current M, i.e., asymptotic exponential reduction. That's okay: each iteration aims to reduce current M by mult (e.g., 0.999). If not, break. This is a chasing target. If M reduces slowly, cap_cur updates. Good. If no improvement, break to next mult lower.
But if mult=0.999, cap = 0.999 M. T = projection reducing peaks to 0.999M; sum h changes tiny. EM step may reduce M not all the way to cap. But we accept if any reduction. After many iterations, M decays. Good. When mult lowers, stronger.

```
def linprog_direction(C):
    try:
       from scipy.optimize import linprog
       n=C.shape[1]
       c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1
       A_ub = np.hstack((C, -np.ones((C.shape[0],1))))
       b_ub = np.zeros(C.shape[0])
       A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1
       b_eq=np.array([1.0])
       bounds=[(0,None)]*n + [(None,None)]
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-8? })
       if res.success: g=res.x[:n]; return norm(max(g,0))
    except: return None
```
Need no `from` inside? Can import top: `from scipy.optimize import linprog` with try. If not, `linprog=None`.

Now, refine each function.

#### norm_sum1
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
       return np.ones_like(a)/max(a.size,1)
    return a/s
```

#### fast_conv
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
    L=2*n-1
    size=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,size)[:L]
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    # optional renormalize sum to 1? FFT error maybe sum !=1. We can scale by sum? Since p sum=1, exact conv sum=1. But FFT errors can make sum slightly off. For M, scaling could introduce bias. Use if s>0: out *= (1.0/s) ? Could enforce sum=1, improving numerical consistency. Fast conv should have sum p²=1. If we scale, M might under/over by tiny. Good to scale.
    s=out.sum()
    if s>1e-15: out = out / s
    return out
```
Should we scale? The objective using evaluate_sequence computes direct conv not scaled by sum? It uses actual p sum ~1. If p sum normalized to 1 exactly? `norm_sum1` makes sum 1 within float, but FFT sum maybe 0.999999999999. Scaling to 1 may correct. For target caps avg=1/L, scaling good. However if p contains negative? no. Do it.
But if p not normalized in `fast_conv` used for candidates? We always normalize before. Good.

#### cross_lag
```
def cross_lag(w,p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64); n=p.size
    L=2*n-1
    if w.size != L: w = np.resize? Not; assume.
    total=L+n-1
    size=1 << (total-1).bit_length()
    r=np.asarray(p[::-1],dtype=np.float64)
    R=np.fft.rfft(r,size); W=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
    out=np.fft.irfft(R*W,size)
    c=out[n-1:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
Need if w has small negative? ratio nonnegative, mirror weights nonnegative. Use max.
Could `r` be view non-contiguous? `p[::-1]` then np.asarray okay. Use `np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])`.

#### project_to_cap
Ensure h nonnegative and sum ~1. If h sum >0 scale to 1? The projection target sum should be 1. In EM, h from fast_conv scaled sum=1. But for safety:
```
h=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64); h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
hs=h.sum()
if hs<1e-15: return np.full_like(h,1.0/len(h))
h=h/hs
L=h.size; avg=1.0/L
cap=max(float(cap), eps)
if cap >= h.max() - 1e-18: return h.copy()
if cap <= avg + 1e-15: return np.full(L, avg)
lo = -float(h.max()) - 1.0
hi=0.0
for _ in range(45):
   mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
   s=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,cap).sum()
   if s < 1.0:
      hi=mid
   else:
      lo=mid
T=np.clip(h-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,cap)
s=T.sum()
if s>1e-15: T=T/s
else: T=np.full(L,avg)
return T
```
But bisection condition: sum(mid) <1 -> need more negative mid (loen lower) or hi? We set hi=mid (upper bound) if sum too low? Let's verify. lo very negative sum high, hi 0 sum low. We need sum=1. If sum(mid) <1, mid too high (less negative), so hi=mid. If sum(mid) >1, mid too low, lo=mid. Good. At end `0.5(lo+hi)` with lo sum high, hi sum low. Good.

#### em_target_step
```
def em_target_step(p,T,alphas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8)):
    p=norm_sum1(p)
    T=np.asarray(T,dtype=np.float64)
    if T.size != 2*len(p)-1: return p, fast_conv(p).max()
    h=fast_conv(p); M=float(h.max())
    hsafe=np.maximum(h,1e-300)
    ratio=np.maximum(T,1e-300)/hsafe
    ratio=np.clip(ratio,1e-12,1e12)
    c=cross_lag(ratio,p)
    c=np.maximum(c,1e-300)
    lc=np.log(c)
    lm=float(np.dot(p,lc)) # p sum 1
    step=lc-lm
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
       return p,M
    best_p=p; best_M=M
    for al in alphas:
       # exponent max al; if al too large > 5, clip al to 6? alphas up to 0.8. If step can have negative large, exp -.8 okay.
       p2=p*np.exp(al*step)
       p2=np.maximum(p2,0.0)
       s=float(p2.sum())
       if s<1e-15: continue
       p2=p2/s
       M2=float(fast_conv(p2).max())
       if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
          best_M=M2; best_p=p2
    return best_p,best_M
```
Sign positive. If T<h at peaks, c maybe lower for positions contributing to peaks; positive update reduces them? Actually p_i multiplied by c_i. If c_i low, it reduces relative to high. Good. Magnitude: step log(c)-logweighted. If c has small variation, alpha effect small.
Could allow `alphas` dynamic no improve. Good.

Potential issue: If p has zeros, `p*exp` stays zero. To recover zero support, we can add tiny floor before update: `p = p + 1e-20/n` normalized. At start of em phase, set `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18/n)`. This makes all entries positive. Do in optimizers: `p = norm_sum1(np.maximum(p, 1e-18/n))`.

def mirror_balance(p0,limit_s,verbose=True):

#### coordinate_polish

```
def coordinate_polish(p0,limit_s,verbose=True):
   token...
   p=norm...; best; it=0; no=0
   rng=np.random.default_rng(77)
   while time:
      b=fast_conv(p); M=... update
      top=np.argsort(b)[::-1][:20]
      improved=False
      # choose 8 random attempts
      for _ in range(8):
         k=int(top[int(rng.integers(len(top)))])
         s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
         if s>e: continue
         idx=np.arange(s,e+1)
         contrib=p[idx]*p[k-idx]
         if contrib.max() < 1e-20: continue
         src=int(idx[np.argmax(contrib)])
         if p[src] <= 1e-12: continue
         scores=np.zeros(n)
         in_peak=np.zeros(n,dtype=bool)
         for q in top:
            qi=int(q); s2=max(0,qi-n+1); e2=min(n-1,qi); if s2<=e2:
               idx2=np.arange(s2,e2+1); scores[idx2]+=p[qi-idx2]; in_peak[s2:e2+1]=True
         cand_mask=~in_peak
         if cand_mask.sum() < n//5: cand_mask=np.ones(n,dtype=bool)
         cand=np.where(cand_mask)[0]
         src_idx=int(np.where(cand==src)[0][0])? But we just need dst argmin scores among cand; if src in cand, skip by setting scores[src]=inf.
         scores[src]=np.inf
         dst=int(cand[np.argmin(scores[cand])])
         if dst == src: continue
         for frac in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1):
            amt=p[src]*frac
            if amt<1e-15: continue
            p2=p.copy(); p2[src]-=amt; p2[dst]+=amt; p2=norm_sum1(p2)
            M2=fast_conv(p2).max()
            if M2 < best_M - 1e-16 or M2 < M - 1e-16:
                p=p2; if M2<best_M update best; improved=True; break
         if improved: break
      if not improved:
          no+=1
          if no>300: break
          if no %50 ==0:
             # random transfer
             i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n)); if i!=j and p[i]>1e-12: transfer small; eval accept.
      it+=1; print
   return best,bestM
```

#### update_best

def update_best(p_best, M_best, candidate, label, t0):
   if candidate is None: return p_best,M_best
   p=norm_sum1(candidate)
   if p.size==0: return
   M=float(fast_conv(p).max())
   if np.isfinite(M) and M < M_best - 1e-15:
       print("NEW BEST [%s] n=%d fast_score=%.9f t=%.0f" % (label,p.size,2*p.size*M,time.monotonic()-t0))

   return p_best,M_best

def phase_time(rem, frac, cap):

#### lp_fw

def get_lp_solver(): ...
def build_constraint_matrix(y, active, n): ...
def linprog_direction(C): ...

def lp_fw(p0, limit_s, solver=None, verbose=True):
   if limit_s<=0: return norm_sum1(p0), fast_conv(...).max()
   deadline=time.monotonic()+limit_s
   y=norm_sum1(p0); n=len(y); best=y; bestM=fast_conv(y).max()
   if solver is None:
      solver = get_lp_solver()
   use_cp = cp is not None and solver is not None
   use_sp = linprog is not None and not use_cp
   if not use_cp and not use_sp: return best,bestM
   b=fast_conv(y); active=sorted(np.argsort(b)[::-1][:max(50, min(300, n//20))].tolist())
   max_K = int(min(800, max(100, 3_000_000//max(n,1))))
   add_per_iter=30
   no=0; it=0
   while time<deadline:
      b=fast_conv(y); M=float(b.max())
      if M < bestM - 1e-16: update
      # trim active
      if len(active)>max_K:
         # keep lags with highest b
         order=np.argsort(np.array([b[k] for k in active]))[::-1]
         active=[active[i] for i in order[:max_K]]
      if len(active)==0: break
      C=build_constraint_matrix(y, active, n)
      g=None
      if use_cp:
         gv=cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True); Mv=cp.Variable()
         cons=[cp.sum(gv)==1, C @ gv <= Mv]
         prob=cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(Mv), cons)
         try: prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
         except: prob=None
         if prob.status in ...:
            val=np.asarray(prob.variables? g_val). 
      if g is None and use_sp:
         g=linprog_direction(C)
      if g is None:
         active=active[:max(50,len(active)//3)]
         continue
      g=norm_sum1(g)
      # line search
      trial_y=y; trial_M=M
      alphas=[1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15,0.05, max(0.01,1.0/(it+2.0))]
      # unique descending? maybe use list and set
      for a in alphas:
         y2=(1-a)*y + a*g; y2=norm_sum1(y2)
         M2=float(fast_conv(y2).max())
         if M2 < trial_M:
            trial_M=M2; trial_y=y2
      # active update from trial_y: b2=fast_conv(trial_y) (we have for best? compute if not)
      b2=fast_conv(trial_y)
      # add top lags
      top2=np.argsort(b2)[::-1][:add_per_iter]
      newset=set(active)
      for k in top2: newset.add(int(k))
      # also keep top max_K by b2
      top_keep=np.argsort(b2)[::-1][:max_K]
      arr=np.array(sorted(newset))
      # maybe trim to top b2
      if len(arr)>max_K:
         order=np.argsort(b2[arr])[::-1]
         arr=arr[order[:max_K]]
      active=sorted(set(int(k) for k in arr))
      y=trial_y
      if trial_M < M - 1e-16: no=0 else no+=1
      if no>200: break
      it+=1; print every 20
   return best,bestM
```

def build_constraint_matrix(y, active, n):
    K=len(active); C=np.zeros((K,n),dtype=np.float64)
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
       s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n,k+1)
       if s<e:
          C[r,s:e]=y[k-np.arange(s,e)]
    return C

`fourier_project(p, basis)`:
```
def fourier_project(p,basis):
   logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
   lm=float(np.dot(p,logp)); centered=logp-lm
   # A = B.T (B*p)
   Bp = basis * p[:,None]
   A = basis.T @ Bp
   D=A.shape[1]
   A += 1e-8*np.eye(D)
   rhs = basis.T @ (p*centered)
   try: coeffs=np.linalg.solve(A,rhs)
   except: coeffs=np.linalg.lstsq(A,rhs,rcond=None)[0]
   return coeffs
```
Memory `basis * p[:,None]` = 2M floats ~16MB; okay.

`fourier_objective`: Use `_FOURIER_CTX`. It should not be called after `propose_candidate` with old context? Set `stop=False`, deadline. If basis missing return `1e100`.
```
def fourier_objective(coeffs):
   ctx=_FOURIER_CTX
   if ctx.get('stop',False): raise _Timeout()
   if time.monotonic() > ctx.get('deadline', np.inf): raise _Timeout()
   B=ctx.get('basis')
   if B is None: return 1e100
   s=B @ coeffs
   s=s-np.max(s); p=np.exp(s); p/=p.sum()
   b=fast_conv(p); M=float(b.max())
   if np.isfinite(M) and M < ctx.get('best_M', np.inf) - 1e-16:
       ctx['best_M']=M; ctx['best_p']=p.copy()
   tau=float(ctx.get('tau', 20.0/max(M,1e-12)))
   z=tau*b; zmax=float(z.max()); z=z-zmax; z=np.clip(z,-50,0.0)
   se=float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
   H=(zmax + np.log(se))/tau
   if not np.isfinite(H): return 1e100
   return float(H)
```
Need compute tau before? Use M. If M 0, tau huge. okay.

`fourier_gradient`:
```
def fourier_gradient(coeffs):
   ctx...; check; B; p; b; update best? maybe not to save? Could update.
   tau; z; sumexp; w=exp(z)/sumexp
   c=cross_lag(w,p)
   centered = c - float(np.dot(p,c))
   grad = B.T @ (p * (2.0*centered))
   if not all finite: grad=np.zeros(B.shape[1])
   return grad
```
We recompute z; okay. Could store p in ctx from objective to avoid recompute, but L-BFGS may call gradient after objective; we can store `ctx['last_p']=p`, `ctx['last_w']=w` maybe. But separate, recompute fine.

### Scheduler details in propose_candidate

Let's write robust:
```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: bud=float(budget_s)
    except: bud=1800.0
    # relase 60 sec final
    total_budget=min(bud-60.0, 1740.0)
    if total_budget < 60: total_budget=max(30.0, bud-5.0)
    deadline=t0+total_budget
    parent=get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is None:
        p=make_fallback(6700)
        parent=None
    else:
        p=parent.copy()
        if p.size > 9000:
            p_large=p
            p=interpolate_seq(p_large, min(7000, max(3000,p.size-1000)))
    p=norm_sum1(np.maximum(p, 1e-18/max(len(p),1)))

#### Function `get_lp_solver`

```
def get_lp_solver():
    global _LP_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None
    if _LP_SOLVER is not None:
        return _LP_SOLVER
    names = ['CLARABEL', 'PDLP', 'ECOS', 'SCS']
    for name in names:
        try:
            solver = getattr(cp, name)
            x = cp.Variable(20, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1])
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ('optimal', 'optimal_inaccurate'):
                _LP_SOLVER = solver
                print("  LP solver: " + str(solver))
                return solver
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        _LP_SOLVER = cp.ECOS
        print("  LP solver fallback ECOS")
    except Exception:
        _LP_SOLVER = None
    return _LP_SOLVER
```
If `cp.SCS` not? getattr returns error object? If AttributeError? Actually cvxpy has SCS. If solver doesn't exist, except. Good. But if test solve returns None status? skip.

In `lp_fw`, if `solver` is something but not? If get returns None. Good.

#### `linprog_direction`

```
def linprog_direction(C):
    if linprog is None: return None
    n=C.shape[1]
    try:
       c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
       A_ub=np.hstack((C, -np.ones((C.shape[0],1))))
       b_ub=np.zeros(C.shape[0])
       A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
       b_eq=np.array([1.0])
       bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n + [(None, None)]
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       if res is not None and res.success:
          g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)[:n]
          g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
          s=g.sum()
          if s>1e-15: return g/s
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
`[(0.0,None)]*n` creates list of tuples okay. No lambda.
Potential if method highscorrect? If method not supported, exception. Good.

#### exact score function

```
def score_of(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
       a=(a/ s if s>0 else a) * 1.0  # to pass? But final candidates sum1.
    a=a/s if s>0 else a
    lst=[float(x) for x in a]
    for x in lst: if not finite return inf
    try: return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception: return np.inf
```
Evaluate_sequence itself clamps negatives; but if a sum<0.01? We normalized sum1. Good. If all zeros, return inf.
Need if evaluate_sequence not defined? It is imported. If not, assume? Not.

#### fallback make function with random smoothing

```
def make_fallback(n=6500):
    n=int(max(1000, min(int(n), 9000)))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cands=[]
    cands.append(np.sin(np.pi*x)**2)
    cands.append(1.0+np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    cands.append(1.0-np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    cands.append(1.0/np.sqrt(np.maximum(x*(1.0-x),1e-6)))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    for _ in range(3):
       u=rng.standard_normal(n)
       kernel=np.ones(max(3, n//500))/max(3,n//500)
       u=np.convolve(u, kernel, mode='same')
       c=np.exp(0.8*u - 0.8*u.max())
       cands.append(c)
    best=None; bestM=np.inf
    for c in cands:
       p=norm_sum1(c)
       M=float(fast_conv(p).max())
       if M<bestM: bestM=M; best=p
    return best
```
`1+cos` nonnegative? min at x=0.5:0, okay. `1-cos` zero at endpoints. Good.

#### `interpolate_seq`

```
def interpolate_seq(f,new_n):
    f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64)
    old_n=f.size
    if old_n==0: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    s=f.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    f=f/s
    if old_n==new_n: return f.copy()
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    g=np.interp(new_x,old_x,f)
    g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
    s=g.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
    return g/s
```

def em_cap_optimize(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+float(limit_s)
    if limit_s <= 0: return norm_sum1(p0), 0.0
    p=norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0,dtype=np.float64))
    n=p.size
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-18/max(1,n)); p=norm_sum1(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_M=float(fast_conv(p).max())
    L=2*n-1; avg=1.0/L
    mults=...
    it=0
    for mult in mults:
       no=0
       while time.monotonic() < deadline:
          h=fast_conv(p); M=float(h.max())
          if M < best_M - 1e-16:
             best_M=M; best=p.copy(); no=0
          cap_cur=max(avg*1.0005, M*float(mult))
          if cap_cur >= M*(1.0-1e-10):
             break
          T=project_to_cap(h, cap_cur)
          alphas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
          p2,M2=em_target_step(p,T,alphas,h,M)
          it +=1
          if M2 < M - 1e-16:
             p=p2; no=0
          else:
             no +=1
             if no > 60: break
          if verbose and it % 100 ==0:
             print("  EMcap mult=%.3f M=%.10g iters=%d t=%.0f" % (...))
    if verbose: print(...)
    return best,best_M

#### `mirror_balance` code details

```
def mirror_balance(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
   t0... p=norm; p=max p floor
   best...; it=0; no=0; tau_idx=0
   tau_factors=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5)
   alphas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(2024)
   while time:
      b=fast_conv(p); M=b.max(); update
      if M < 1e-300: break
      med=float(np.median(b)); spread=max(M-med, M*1e-8)
      tau=tau_factors[tau_idx % len(tau_factors)] * spread
      if tau <=0: break
      z=(b-M)/tau; z=np.clip(z,-50,0.0)
      w=np.exp(z); ws=w.sum(); if ws<=0: break; w/=ws
      c=cross_lag(w,p)
      d=c - float(np.average(c, weights=p))
      scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
      if scale < 1e-15:
          no +=1
          if no>500: break
          tau_idx +=1
          continue
      d=d/scale
      best_trial=p; best_trial_M=M
      for a in alphas:
         p2=p*np.exp(-a*d)
         p2=np.maximum(p2,0.0); s2=p2.sum(); if s2<1e-15: continue
         p2=p2/s2
         M2=float(fast_conv(p2).max())
         if M2 < best_trial_M - 1e-16:
            best_trial=M2; best_trial_p=p2
      # if no direct improve, try power softmax
      if best_trial_M >= M - 1e-16:
         for beta in (0.2,0.5,1.0):
            logp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)) - beta*d
            logp -= logp.max()
            p2=np.exp(logp); p2/=p2.sum()
            M2=...
            if M2 < best_trial_M - 1e-16: ...
      if best_trial_M < M - 1e-16:
          p=best_trial_p; no=0
      else:
          no +=1
          if no % 20 ==0: tau_idx +=1
          if no > 800:
             noise=0.02*rng.normal(0,1,n)*best
             p=norm_sum1(best+noise); no=0; tau_idx=0
      it +=1
      if verbose and it%200==0: print
   return best,best_M

```
def coordinate_polish(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
   t0...
   p=norm...; best...
   n=p.size
   rng=np.random.default_rng(99)
   it=0; no=0
   while time:
      b=fast_conv(p); M=... update
      top=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
      top=top[:max(1,min(20,n*2-1))]
      improved=False
      for _ in range(3):
         k=int(top[int(rng.integers(len(top)))])
         s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
         if s>e: continue
         idx=np.arange(s,e+1)
         contrib=p[idx]*p[k-idx]
         if float(contrib.max()) < 1e-20: continue
         src=int(idx[int(np.argmax(contrib))])
         if p[src] <= 1e-12: continue
         # scores
         scores=np.zeros(n)
         in_peak=np.zeros(n,dtype=bool)
         for qv in top[:10]:
             qi=int(qv)
             s2=max(0,qi-n+1); e2=min(n-1,qi)
             if s2<=e2:
                 idx2=np.arange(s2,e2+1)
                 scores[idx2]+=p[qi-idx2]
                 in_peak[s2:e2+1]=True
         scores[src]=np.inf
         if int(np.count_nonzero(~in_peak)) < n//5:
             candidates=np.arange(n)
         else:
             candidates=np.where(~in_peak)[0]
         dst=int(candidates[int(np.argmin(scores[candidates]))])
         if dst == src: continue
         for frac in (0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05):
             amt=p[src]*frac
             if amt <= 1e-15: continue
             p2=p.copy(); p2[src]-=amt; p2[dst]+=amt
             p2=norm_sum1(p2)
             M2=float(fast_conv(p2).max())
             if M2 < best_M - 1e-16 or M2 < M - 1e-16:
                 p=p2
                 if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
                    best_M=M2; best=p2.copy()
                 improved=True
                 break
         if improved: break
      if not improved:
         no +=1
         if no > 250: break
         if no % 50 ==0:
             i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
             if i!=j and p[i]>1e-12:
                p2=p.copy(); amt=p[i]*0.005; p2[i]-=amt; p2[j]+=amt; p2=norm_sum1(p2); M2=...
                if M2 < M -1e-16: p=p2; if M2<best update
      it+=1
      if verbose and it%200==0: print
   return best,best_M
```

### Let's implement `fourier_run` exactly:
```
def fourier_run(B, coeffs, deadline, tau, maxiter, init_p, init_M):
    if minimize is None: return init_p, init_M
    ctx=_FOURIER_CTX
    ctx.clear()
    ctx['basis']=B; ctx['tau']=float(tau); ctx['deadline']=deadline; ctx['stop']=False
    if init_p is not None:
       ctx['best_p']=np.asarray(init_p,dtype=np.float64).copy(); ctx['best_M']=float(init_M)
    else:
       ctx['best_p']=None; ctx['best_M']=np.inf
    options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-13, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':40}
    try:
        minimize(fourier_objective, np.asarray(coeffs), jac=fourier_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', options=options)
    except _Timeout:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass
    return ctx.get('best_p'), ctx.get('best_M')
```
Need if `coeffs` array reused; minimize may modify? It passes. We can pass copy. Good.

def cap_target_step(p,T,alphas,h,M,use_em_backup=True):

#### `fast_conv` bit_length: if L=0? no.
```
def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:L]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 1e-15:
        out /= s
    return out
```
If p contains inf? no. If A*A complex, irfft. Good.

#### `cross_lag`
```
def cross_lag(w, p, nonneg=True):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != L:
        w = np.resize(w, L)
    total = L + n - 1
    size = 1 << (total - 1).bit_length()
    r = np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    R = np.fft.rfft(r, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(W * R, size)
    c = out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if nonneg:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
If w size mismatch, `np.resize` could silently; but assume. `np.resize` OK no lambda.

#### `project_to_cap` as above.

#### `em_target_step`, `targeted_gradient_step`

Be careful variable names in `targeted_gradient_step`:
```
def targeted_gradient_step(p, T, alphas, h=None, M=None):
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    T = np.asarray(T,dtype=np.float64)
    if h is None:
       h=fast_conv(p); M=float(h.max())
    if M is None: M=float(h.max())
    resid=h-T
    c=cross_lag(resid,p,nonneg=False)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    d=c - float(np.average(c, weights=p))
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
       return p, M
    d=d/scale
    best_p=p; best_M=M
    for a in alphas:
       p2=p*np.exp(-a*d)
       p2=np.maximum(p2,0.0)
       s2=p2.sum()
       if s2<1e-15: continue
       p2=p2/s2
       M2=float(fast_conv(p2).max())
       if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
          best_M=M2; best_p=p2
    return best_p,best_M
```
If p has zeros, p2 remains zeros; okay. We might want floor to allow new support: before exp, `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18/p.size)`? In optimizers p already floored. `norm_sum1` no floor. But after many updates, p remains positive if initial positive. Good.
`np.average` with weights p where sum 1; if p all zero? no.

`em_target_step` similar but positive sign and nonneg cross.

#### `em_cap_optimize` code with gradient+EM

```
def em_cap_optimize(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+float(limit_s)
   if limit_s <=0:
       p=norm_sum1(p0); return p,float(fast_conv(p).max())
   p=norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0,dtype=np.float64))
   n=p.size
   p=np.maximum(p, 1e-18/max(1,n)); p=norm_sum1(p)
   best=p.copy(); best_M=float(fast_conv(p).max())
   L=2*n-1; avg=1.0/L
   mults=(0.9995,0.999,0.9985,0.998,0.997,0.995,0.99,0.98,0.97,0.95,0.93,0.9,0.85,0.8,0.75)
   alphas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5)
   it=0
   for mult in mults:
       no=0
       while time.monotonic() < deadline:
           h=fast_conv(p); M=float(h.max())
           if M < best_M - 1e-16:
               best_M=M; best=p.copy(); no=0
           cap_cur=max(avg*1.0005, M*float(mult))
           if cap_cur >= M*(1.0-1e-10):
               break
           T=project_to_cap(h, cap_cur)
           p_try= target_grad? (p_g,M_g=targeted_gradient_step(...))
           accepted=False
           if M_g < M - 1e-16:
               p=p_g; accepted=True; no=0
           else:
               if it % 3 == 0:
                   p_e,M_e=em_target_step(...)
                   if M_e < M - 1e-16:
                      p=p_e; accepted=True; no=0
               if not accepted:
                   no +=1
                   if no > 250: break
           it +=1
           if verbose and it % 200 ==0: print
   if verbose: print
   return best,best_M
```
Need variable `p_g`, `M_g`. If M_g< M but also maybe less than best? update at next loop. Good. If both no, no. If `cap_cur` close to M but target step M_g maybe unchanged. Break.
`avg*1.0005` ensures cap > avg. If n small maybe.
If M very small? no.

```python
"""
Searches for highly flat nonnegative linear-convolution sequences.

Objective: minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The search combines:
- cap-targeted projected-quadratic updates with a projected target h,
- EM-style multiplicative target matching,
- softmax-weighted mirror descent over near-maximum convolution peaks,
- Frank-Wolfe/LP direction search with active sets,
- low-dimensional Fourier BFGS refinement,
- greedy coordinate transfers,
- symmetry interpolation checks and final exact validation.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import cvxpy as cp
except Exception:
    cp = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    minimize = None
    linprog = None


class _Timeout(Exception):
    pass


_LP_SOLVER = None
_FOURIER_CTX = {}


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:L]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s > 1e-15:
        out = out / s
    return out


def cross_lag(w, p, nonneg=True):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != L:
        w = np.resize(w, L)
    total = L + n - 1
    size = 1 << (total - 1).bit_length()
    r = np.ascontiguousarray(p[::-1])
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    R = np.fft.rfft(r, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(W * R, size)
    c = out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if nonneg:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def project_to_cap(h, cap):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    hs = float(h.sum())
    if hs < 1e-15:
        return np.full(h.size, 1.0 / max(1, h.size), dtype=np.float64)
    h = h / hs
    L = h.size
    cap = float(cap)
    avg = 1.0 / L
    if cap <= avg + 1e-15:
        return np.full(L, avg, dtype=np.float64)
    hmax = float(h.max())
    if cap >= hmax - 1e-18:
        return h.copy()

    lo = -hmax - 1.0
    hi = 0.0
    for _ in range(30):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, cap).sum())
        if s < 1.0:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    T = np.clip(h - lam, 0.0, cap)
    s = float(T.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        T = np.full(L, avg, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        T = T / s
    return T


def em_target_step(p, T, alphas, h=None, M=None):
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    T = np.asarray(T, dtype=np.float64)
    if h is None:
        h = fast_conv(p)
        M = float(h.max())
    hsafe = np.maximum(h, 1e-300)
    ratio = np.maximum(T, 1e-300) / hsafe
    ratio = np.clip(ratio, 1e-12, 1e12)
    c = cross_lag(ratio, p, nonneg=True)
    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
    lc = np.log(c)
    lm = float(np.dot(p, lc))
    step = lc - lm
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
        return p, M
    best_p = p
    best_M = M
    for a in alphas:
        p2 = p * np.exp(float(a) * step)
        p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
        s2 = float(p2.sum())
        if s2 < 1e-15:
            continue
        p2 = p2 / s2
        M2 = float(fast_conv(p2).max())
        if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M2
            best_p = p2
    return best_p, best_M


def targeted_gradient_step(p, T, alphas, h=None, M=None):
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    T = np.asarray(T, dtype=np.float64)
    if h is None:
        h = fast_conv(p)
        M = float(h.max())
    resid = h - T
    c = cross_lag(resid, p, nonneg=False)
    d = c - float(np.dot(p, c))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
        return p, M
    d = d / scale
    best_p = p
    best_M = M
    for a in alphas:
        p2 = p * np.exp(-float(a) * d)
        p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
        s2 = float(p2.sum())
        if s2 < 1e-15:
            continue
        p2 = p2 / s2
        M2 = float(fast_conv(p2).max())
        if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M2
            best_p = p2
    return best_p, best_M


def em_cap_optimize(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(limit_s)
    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18 / float(n))
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(p).max())
    L = 2 * n - 1
    avg = 1.0 / L
    mults = (
        0.9995, 0.9992, 0.999, 0.9988, 0.9985, 0.998, 0.997, 0.995,
        0.99, 0.98, 0.97, 0.95, 0.93, 0.90, 0.85, 0.80, 0.75
    )
    alphas = (0.005, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 0.8)
    it = 0

    for mult in mults:
        no = 0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            h = fast_conv(p)
            M = float(h.max())
            if M < best_M - 1e-16:
                best_M = M
                best = p.copy()
                no = 0

            cap_cur = max(avg * 1.0005, M * float(mult))
            if cap_cur >= M * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                break

            T = project_to_cap(h, cap_cur)
            accepted = False

            pg, Mg = targeted_gradient_step(p, T, alphas, h, M)
            if Mg < M - 1e-16:
                p = pg
                accepted = True
                no = 0

            if not accepted and it % 3 == 0:
                pe, Me = em_target_step(p, T, alphas, h, M)
                if Me < M - 1e-16:
                    p = pe
                    accepted = True
                    no = 0

            if not accepted:
                no += 1
                if no > 120:
                    break

            it += 1
            if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
                print("  EMcap mult=%.4f fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
                      (mult, 2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))

    if verbose:
        print("  EMcap done fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
              (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return best, best_M


def em_flat_optimize(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(limit_s)
    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18 / float(n))
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(p).max())
    L = 2 * n - 1
    T = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    alphas = (0.005, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 0.8)
    it = 0
    no = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        h = fast_conv(p)
        M = float(h.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M
            best = p.copy()
            no = 0

        pg, Mg = targeted_gradient_step(p, T, alphas, h, M)
        if Mg < M - 1e-16:
            p = pg
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
            if no > 350:
                break
            if no % 100 == 0:
                p = best.copy()

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print("  EMflat fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
                  (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))

    if verbose:
        print("  EMflat done fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
              (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return best, best_M


def mirror_balance(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(limit_s)
    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18 / float(n))
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(p).max())

    tau_factors = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.50)
    alphas = (0.005, 0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 0.8, 1.2)
    it = 0
    no = 0
    tau_idx = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(2024)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = fast_conv(p)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M
            best = p.copy()
            no = 0

        if M <= 1e-300:
            break

        med = float(np.median(b))
        spread = max(M - med, M * 1e-8)
        tau = tau_factors[tau_idx % len(tau_factors)] * spread
        if tau <= 0.0:
            break

        z = (b - M) / tau
        z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(z)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 0.0:
            break
        w = w / ws

        c = cross_lag(w, p, nonneg=True)
        mean_c = float(np.dot(p, c))
        d = c - mean_c
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale < 1e-15:
            no += 1
            if no > 500:
                break
            tau_idx += 1
            continue
        d = d / scale

        trial_p = p
        trial_M = M

        for a in alphas:
            p2 = p * np.exp(-float(a) * d)
            p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
            s2 = float(p2.sum())
            if s2 < 1e-15:
                continue
            p2 = p2 / s2
            M2 = float(fast_conv(p2).max())
            if M2 < trial_M - 1e-16:
                trial_M = M2
                trial_p = p2

        if trial_M >= M - 1e-16:
            for beta in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0):
                logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)) - float(beta) * d
                logp = logp - float(np.max(logp))
                p2 = np.exp(logp)
                s2 = float(p2.sum())
                if s2 < 1e-15:
                    continue
                p2 = p2 / s2
                M2 = float(fast_conv(p2).max())
                if M2 < trial_M - 1e-16:
                    trial_M = M2
                    trial_p = p2

        if trial_M < M - 1e-16:
            p = trial_p
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
            if no % 20 == 0:
                tau_idx += 1
            if no > 700:
                noise = 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n) * best
                p = norm_sum1(best + noise)
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18 / float(n))
                p = norm_sum1(p)
                no = 0
                tau_idx = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 300 == 0:
            print("  Mirror fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
                  (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))

    if verbose:
        print("  Mirror done fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
              (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return best, best_M


def symmetrize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    q = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return norm_sum1(p)
    return q / s


def interpolate_seq(f, new_n):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    old_n = f.size
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    s = float(f.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f / s
    f = f / s
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    g = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s2 = float(g.sum())
    if s2 < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    return g / s2


def get_parent_sequence():
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            arr = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
            arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0:
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if np.isfinite(arr).all() and float(arr.sum()) > 1e-10:
                    return norm_sum1(arr)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


def make_fallback(n=6500):
    n = int(max(1000, min(int(n), 9000)))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cands = []
    cands.append(np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2)
    cands.append(1.0 + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    cands.append(1.0 - np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    cands.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.maximum(x * (1.0 - x), 1e-6)))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(123)
    k = max(3, n // 500)
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    for _ in range(4):
        u = rng.standard_normal(n)
        u = np.convolve(u, kernel, mode="same")
        c = np.exp(0.8 * u - 0.8 * float(np.max(u)))
        cands.append(c)

    best = None
    best_M = np.inf
    for c in cands:
        p = norm_sum1(c)
        M = float(fast_conv(p).max())
        if M < best_M:
            best_M = M
            best = p
    return best


def score_of(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or a.size > 20000:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    try:
        lst = []
        for v in a:
            v = float(v)
            if not np.isfinite(v):
                return np.inf
            lst.append(v)
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf


def update_best(p_best, M_best, candidate, label, t0):
    if candidate is None:
        return p_best, M_best
    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(candidate, dtype=np.float64))
    if p.size == 0:
        return p_best, M_best
    M = float(fast_conv(p).max())
    if np.isfinite(M) and M < M_best - 1e-15:
        print("NEW BEST [%s] n=%d fast_score=%.9f t=%.0f" %
              (label, p.size, 2.0 * p.size * M, time.monotonic() - t0))
        return p.copy(), M
    return p_best, M_best


def phase_time(rem, frac, cap):
    if rem <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return float(max(0.0, min(float(cap), float(rem) * float(frac), float(rem) - 5.0)))


def coordinate_polish(p0, limit_s, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(limit_s)
    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18 / float(n))
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(p).max())
    rng = np.random.default_rng(99)
    it = 0
    no = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = fast_conv(p)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M
            best = p.copy()

        top = np.argsort(b)[::-1][:max(1, min(20, 2 * n - 1))]
        improved = False

        for _ in range(3):
            k = int(top[int(rng.integers(len(top)))])
            s = max(0, k - n + 1)
            e = min(n - 1, k)
            if s > e:
                continue
            idx = np.arange(s, e + 1)
            contrib = p[idx] * p[k - idx]
            if float(contrib.max()) < 1e-20:
                continue
            src = int(idx[int(np.argmax(contrib))])
            if p[src] <= 1e-12:
                continue

            scores = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            in_peak = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
            for qv in top[:10]:
                qi = int(qv)
                s2 = max(0, qi - n + 1)
                e2 = min(n - 1, qi)
                if s2 <= e2:
                    idx2 = np.arange(s2, e2 + 1)
                    scores[idx2] += p[qi - idx2]
                    in_peak[s2:e2 + 1] = True

            scores[src] = np.inf
            if int(np.count_nonzero(~in_peak)) < n // 5:
                candidates = np.arange(n)
            else:
                candidates = np.where(~in_peak)[0]
            dst = int(candidates[int(np.argmin(scores[candidates]))])
            if dst == src:
                continue

            for frac in (0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05):
                amt = p[src] * frac
                if amt <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                p2 = p.copy()
                p2[src] -= amt
                p2[dst] += amt
                p2 = norm_sum1(p2)
                M2 = float(fast_conv(p2).max())
                if M2 < best_M - 1e-16 or M2 < M - 1e-16:
                    p = p2
                    if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
                        best = p2.copy()
                        best_M = M2
                    improved = True
                    break

            if improved:
                no = 0
                break

        if not improved:
            no += 1
            if no > 220:
                break
            if no % 50 == 0:
                i = int(rng.integers(n))
                j = int(rng.integers(n))
                if i != j and p[i] > 1e-12:
                    amt = p[i] * 0.005
                    p2 = p.copy()
                    p2[i] -= amt
                    p2[j] += amt
                    p2 = norm_sum1(p2)
                    M2 = float(fast_conv(p2).max())
                    if M2 < M - 1e-16:
                        p = p2
                        if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
                            best = p2.copy()
                            best_M = M2
                        no = 0

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
            print("  Coord fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
                  (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))

    if verbose:
        print("  Coord done fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
              (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return best, best_M


def get_lp_solver():
    global _LP_SOLVER
    if cp is None:
        return None
    if _LP_SOLVER is not None:
        return _LP_SOLVER
    names = ("CLARABEL", "PDLP", "ECOS", "SCS")
    for name in names:
        try:
            solver = getattr(cp, name)
            x = cp.Variable(20, nonneg=True)
            prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(cp.sum(x)), [cp.sum(x) == 1])
            prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
            if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                _LP_SOLVER = solver
                print("  LP solver: " + str(solver))
                return solver
        except Exception:
            pass
    _LP_SOLVER = None
    print("  LP solver: none (will use scipy fallback if available)")
    return None


def build_constraint_matrix(y, active, n):
    K = len(active)
    C = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for r, k in enumerate(active):
        s = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
        e = min(n, int(k) + 1)
        if s < e:
            C[r, s:e] = y[int(k) - np.arange(s, e)]
    return C


def linprog_direction(C):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    try:
        n = C.shape[1]
        cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cvec[-1] = 1.0
        A_ub = np.hstack((C, -np.ones((C.shape[0], 1), dtype=np.float64)))
        b_ub = np.zeros(C.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(None, None)]
        res = linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True}
        )
        if res is not None and res.success:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)[:n]
            g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if s > 1e-15:
                return g / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def lp_fw(p0, limit_s, solver=None, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(limit_s)
    y = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = y.size
    if n == 0 or limit_s <= 0:
        return y, float(fast_conv(y).max())

    best_p = y.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(y).max())

    if cp is None and linprog is None:
        return best_p, best_M

    L = 2 * n - 1
    max_K = int(min(350, max(80, 2000000 // max(1, n))))
    if n * max_K > 5000000:
        return best_p, best_M

    b = fast_conv(y)
    initial_K = max(30, min(160, L))
    active = [int(x) for x in np.argsort(b)[::-1][:initial_K].tolist()]

    it = 0
    no = 0
    add_per_iter = 30

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b = fast_conv(y)
        M = float(b.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M
            best_p = y.copy()

        if len(active) > max_K:
            scores = np.array([b[int(k)] for k in active], dtype=np.float64)
            order = np.argsort(scores)[::-1]
            active = [int(active[i]) for i in order[:max_K]]
        if len(active) == 0:
            break

        C = build_constraint_matrix(y, active, n)
        g = None

        if cp is not None and solver is not None:
            try:
                g_var = cp.Variable(n, nonneg=True)
                M_var = cp.Variable()
                cons = [cp.sum(g_var) == 1, C @ g_var <= M_var]
                prob = cp.Problem(cp.Minimize(M_var), cons)
                prob.solve(solver=solver, verbose=False)
                if prob.status in ("optimal", "optimal_inaccurate"):
                    val = g_var.value
                    if val is not None:
                        g = np.asarray(val, dtype=np.float64)
            except Exception:
                g = None

        if g is None and linprog is not None:
            g = linprog_direction(C)

        if g is None:
            active = active[:max(20, len(active) // 3)]
            no += 1
            if no > 50:
                break
            continue

        g = norm_sum1(g)

        alphas = (1.0, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.15, 0.05)
        xs = 1.0 / float(it + 2.0)
        if 0.0 < xs < 0.5:
            alphas = alphas + (float(xs),)

        trial_y = y
        trial_M = M
        for a in alphas:
            y2 = (1.0 - float(a)) * y + float(a) * g
            y2 = norm_sum1(y2)
            M2 = float(fast_conv(y2).max())
            if M2 < trial_M:
                trial_M = M2
                trial_y = y2

        improved = trial_M < M - 1e-16
        if improved:
            y = trial_y
            no = 0
            b2 = fast_conv(y)
            new_set = set(active)
            top2 = np.argsort(b2)[::-1][:add_per_iter]
            for k in top2:
                new_set.add(int(k))
            top_keep = np.argsort(b2)[::-1][:max_K]
            for k in top_keep:
                new_set.add(int(k))
            arr = np.array(sorted(new_set), dtype=int)
            if arr.size > max_K:
                sc = b2[arr]
                order = np.argsort(sc)[::-1]
                arr = arr[order[:max_K]]
            active = [int(x) for x in arr]
            if trial_M < best_M - 1e-16:
                best_M = trial_M
                best_p = y.copy()
        else:
            no += 1
            if no > 120:
                break

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 20 == 0:
            print("  LP-FW iter=%d fast_score=%.9f |S|=%d t=%.0f" %
                  (it, 2.0 * n * best_M, len(active), time.monotonic() - t0))

    if verbose:
        print("  LP-FW done fast_score=%.9f iters=%d t=%.0f" %
              (2.0 * n * best_M, it, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return best_p, best_M


def fourier_build_basis(n, modes):
    modes = int(max(1, modes))
    n = int(n)
    D = 2 * modes
    B = np.empty((n, D), dtype=np.float64)
    theta = (2.0 * np.pi / float(n)) * np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    col = 0
    for m in range(1, modes + 1):
        B[:, col] = np.cos(float(m) * theta)
        col += 1
        B[:, col] = np.sin(float(m) * theta)
        col += 1
    return B


def fourier_project(p, B):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    lm = float(np.dot(p, logp))
    centered = logp - lm
    D = B.shape[1]
    Bp = B * p[:, None]
    A = B.T @ Bp
    A = A + 1e-9 * np.eye(D, dtype=np.float64)
    rhs = B.T @ (p * centered)
    try:
        return np.linalg.solve(A, rhs)
    except Exception:
        return np.linalg.lstsq(A, rhs, rcond=None)[0]


def fourier_objective(coeffs):
    ctx = _FOURIER_CTX
    if ctx.get("stop", False):
        raise _Timeout()
    deadline = ctx.get("deadline", np.inf)
    if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
        ctx["stop"] = True
        raise _Timeout()
    B = ctx.get("basis")
    if B is None:
        return 1e100
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64)
    s = B @ coeffs
    s = s - float(np.max(s))
    p = np.exp(s)
    s2 = float(p.sum())
    if s2 < 1e-15:
        return 1e100
    p = p / s2
    b = fast_conv(p)
    M = float(b.max())
    if np.isfinite(M) and M < float(ctx.get("best_M", np.inf)) - 1e-16:
        ctx["best_M"] = M
        ctx["best_p"] = p.copy()

    tau = float(ctx.get("tau", 20.0 / max(1e-12, M)))
    z = tau * b
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z - zmax, -50.0, 0.0)
    se = float(np.sum(np.exp(z)))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return 1e100
    H = (zmax + float(np.log(se))) / tau
    return float(2.0 * p.size * H)


def fourier_gradient(coeffs):
    ctx = _FOURIER_CTX
    if ctx.get("stop", False):
        raise _Timeout()
    deadline = ctx.get("deadline", np.inf)
    if time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
        ctx["stop"] = True
        raise _Timeout()
    B = ctx.get("basis")
    if B is None:
        return np.array([])
    coeffs = np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64)
    s = B @ coeffs
    s = s - float(np.max(s))
    p = np.exp(s)
    s2 = float(p.sum())
    if s2 < 1e-15:
        return np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s2
    b = fast_conv(p)
    tau = float(ctx.get("tau", 20.0 / max(1e-12, float(b.max()))))
    z = tau * b
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z - zmax, -50.0, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    se = float(np.sum(ez))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        return np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    w = ez / se
    c = cross_lag(w, p, nonneg=True)
    centered = c - float(np.dot(p, c))
    grad = (4.0 * p.size) * (B.T @ (p * centered))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    return grad


def fourier_run(B, coeffs, deadline, tau, maxiter, init_p, init_M):
    if minimize is None:
        return init_p, init_M
    ctx = _FOURIER_CTX
    ctx.clear()
    ctx["basis"] = B
    ctx["tau"] = float(tau)
    ctx["deadline"] = float(deadline)
    ctx["stop"] = False
    if init_p is not None:
        ctx["best_p"] = np.asarray(init_p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        ctx["best_M"] = float(init_M)
    else:
        ctx["best_p"] = None
        ctx["best_M"] = np.inf

    options = {
        "maxiter": int(max(10, maxiter)),
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "maxls": 40
    }
    try:
        minimize(
            fourier_objective,
            np.asarray(coeffs, dtype=np.float64).copy(),
            jac=fourier_gradient,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options=options
        )
    except _Timeout:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass
    return ctx.get("best_p"), ctx.get("best_M")


def fourier_optimize(p0, limit_s, modes=60, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(limit_s)
    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = p.size
    if n == 0 or limit_s < 30 or minimize is None:
        return p, float(fast_conv(p).max())

    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-18 / float(n))
    p = norm_sum1(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(p).max())

    modes_first = int(min(max(8, modes), max(8, n // 40)))
    B = fourier_build_basis(n, modes_first)
    coeffs = fourier_project(best, B)

    p1, M1 = fourier_run(B, coeffs, deadline, max(1.0, 10.0 / max(best_M, 1e-12)), 350, best, best_M)
    if M1 < best_M - 1e-16:
        best = p1.copy()
        best_M = M1

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40:
        modes_second = int(min(max(modes_first + 20, 32), max(8, n // 30)))
        B2 = fourier_build_basis(n, modes_second)
        coeffs2 = fourier_project(best, B2)
        p2, M2 = fourier_run(B2, coeffs2, deadline, max(1.0, 40.0 / max(best_M, 1e-12)), 220, best, best_M)
        if M2 < best_M - 1e-16:
            best = p2.copy()
            best_M = M2

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(555)
        scale = float(np.linalg.norm(coeffs))
        if scale < 1e-12:
            scale = 0.1
        rand_coeffs = coeffs + 0.25 * scale * rng.standard_normal(coeffs.size)
        p3, M3 = fourier_run(B, rand_coeffs, deadline, max(1.0, 20.0 / max(best_M, 1e-12)), 120, best, best_M)
        if M3 < best_M - 1e-16:
            best = p3.copy()
            best_M = M3

    if verbose:
        print("  Fourier done fast_score=%.9f t=%.0f" %
              (2.0 * n * best_M, time.monotonic() - t0))
    return best, best_M


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    total_budget = min(max(60.0, budget - 60.0), 1740.0)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget

    parent = get_parent_sequence()
    if parent is None:
        p = make_fallback(6700)
    else:
        p = parent.copy()
        if p.size > 9000:
            p = interpolate_seq(p, max(3000, min(7000, p.size - 1000)))

    p = norm_sum1(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))
    best_seq = p.copy()
    best_M = float(fast_conv(best_seq).max())
    print("Initial n=%d fast_score=%.9f" % (best_seq.size, 2.0 * best_seq.size * best_M))
    if score_of(best_seq) == np.inf:
        # If exact validation fails for the initial candidate, keep searching; final fallback below.
        pass

    lp_solver = get_lp_solver()

    schedule = (
        ("em_cap", 0.22, 320),
        ("mirror", 0.16, 240),
        ("lp", 0.22, 360),
        ("em_flat", 0.06, 100),
        ("mirror", 0.10, 150),
        ("fourier", 0.10, 160),
        ("coord", 0.05, 80),
        ("em_cap", 0.12, 180),
        ("mirror", 0.07, 100),
    )

    for mode, frac, cap in schedule:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30:
            break
        lt = phase_time(rem, frac, cap)
        if lt <= 5:
            continue

        if mode == "em_cap":
            cand, _ = em_cap_optimize(best_seq, lt, verbose=True)
        elif mode == "mirror":
            cand, _ = mirror_balance(best_seq, lt, verbose=True)
        elif mode == "em_flat":
            cand, _ = em_flat_optimize(best_seq, lt, verbose=True)
        elif mode == "lp":
            if lp_solver is not None and best_seq.size <= 9000:
                cand, _ = lp_fw(best_seq, lt, solver=lp_solver, verbose=True)
            else:
                cand, _ = em_cap_optimize(best_seq, lt, verbose=True)
        elif mode == "fourier":
            cand, _ = fourier_optimize(best_seq, lt, modes=60, verbose=True)
        elif mode == "coord":
            cand, _ = coordinate_polish(best_seq, lt, verbose=True)
        else:
            cand = best_seq

        best_seq, best_M = update_best(best_seq, best_M, cand, mode, t0)
        sym = symmetrize(best_seq)
        best_seq, best_M = update_best(best_seq, best_M, sym, "sym", t0)

    # Final alternating polish with whatever time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    while rem > 35:
        lt = min(70.0, rem - 20.0)
        cand, _ = mirror_balance(best_seq, lt, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_M = update_best(best_seq, best_M, cand, "final-mirror", t0)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 35:
            break
        lt = min(35.0, rem - 10.0)
        cand, _ = em_cap_optimize(best_seq, lt, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_M = update_best(best_seq, best_M, cand, "final-emcap", t0)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 50:
            break

    # Exact final validation among a small set of candidates.
    final_best = None
    final_score = np.inf

    candidates = []
    if parent is not None and parent.size <= 20000:
        candidates.append(parent)
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 20000:
        candidates.append(best_seq)
    sym = symmetrize(best_seq)
    if best_seq is not None and sym.size <= 20000:
        candidates.append(sym)

    for cand in candidates:
        sc = score_of(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = sc
            final_best = norm_sum1(np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64))

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = norm_sum1(best_seq if best_seq is not None else make_fallback(6000))
        final_score = score_of(final_best)
        if not np.isfinite(final_score):
            final_best = make_fallback(6000)

    out = []
    for v in final_best:
        v = float(v)
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            v = 0.0
        if v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        out.append(v)
    s = float(sum(out))
    if s < 0.01:
        out = [1.0]
    else:
        out = [v / s for v in out]

    exact_final = score_of(out)
    print("FINAL exact_score=%r n=%d time=%.0f" %
          (exact_final, len(out), time.monotonic() - t0))
    return out
```
